ciṟuvaṉ vaḷara vaḷara, avaṉuṭaiya vāḻkkaiyil perum pakuti paṟṟukkaḷāl niṟaikiṉṟaṉa; paḷḷiyai naṭuvākak koṇṭu pala paṟṟukkaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. taṉippaṭṭa āciriyar mītu paṟṟu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. namatu nāṭṭaip poruttamaṭṭilum kuṭumpattiṉ mītu paṟṟu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. ataṉ piṟaku nāṭṭai virumpu kiṉṟōm. ataṉ payaṉāka nāṭṭuk kuḻaittu, aḻiyāp pukaḻpeṟṟa, taṉṉalamaṟṟa vīrarkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷ camaya tūtarkaḷ, camūkac cīrtiruttavātikaḷ, nāṭṭukkoṭi, nāṭṭu vāḻttup pāṭal pōṉṟavaṟṟaip pōṟṟukiṉṟōm. pāratiyāriṉ centamiḻ nāṭeṉum pōtiṉilē eṉṟa pāṭalai īṇṭu niṉaivu kūrka. inta vitamāṉa uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷiṉ cērkkai nāṭṭuppaṟṟaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. intap paṟṟukkaḷaippōlavē, māṇākkarkaḷukkup pāṭaṅkaḷilum paṟṟu amaivatuṇṭu. paṟṟup poruḷaik katiravaṉākavum, uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷaik kōḷkaḷākavum uruvakappaṭutti uraikkalām. kōḷkaḷ katiravaṉaic cuṟṟi varuvaṉa pōlavē, uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ paṟṟup poruḷaic cuṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṭcip poruḷkaḷaic cuṟṟiyum maṉitarkaḷaic cuṟṟiyum paṟṟukkaḷ amaivaṉa pōlavē, karuttup poruḷkaḷaiyum kuṟikkōṭ poruḷkaḷaiyum cuṟṟip paṟṟukkaḷ paṭikiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟai aṟap paṟṟukkaḷ5 eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. aṉpu, nīti, nērmai, uṇmai pōṉṟa karuttup poruḷkaḷaic cuṟṟip paṟṟukkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ nalloḻukkam vaḷarkiṉṟatu. aṟap paṟṟukkaḷ yāvum camūka vāḻkkaiyiṉ viḷaivāl ēṟpaṭupavai. cila camūkattiṟkum, cila camayaṅkaḷukkum, cila caṅkaṅkaḷukkum pala aṟap paṟṟukkaḷ talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyāka vantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. rā6 eṉṟa uḷaviyal aṟiñar aṟappaṟṟukkaḷ kāṭcic ciṟappu, kāṭcip potu, karuttu eṉṟa oḻuṅkil vaḷarvatākak kūṟukiṉṟār. oru ciṟuvaṉ tāṉ vāḻum cūḻnilaiyil uḷḷa kuṟippiṭṭa oruvarmītu aṉpu celuttu kiṉṟāṉ; iṅku ac ciṟuvaṉiṉ aṉpu eṉṉum paṟṟu kāṭcic ciṟappup paṟṟāka amaikiṉṟatu. piṟaku ivaraip pōṉṟa palariṭamum aṉpu celuttukiṉṟāṉ; ippoḻutu ap paṟṟu kāṭcip potup paṟṟāka amai kiṉṟatu. iṟutiyāka, ac ciṟuvaṉ ammāntar aṉaivarum piṉpaṟṟum paṇpiṉmītu aṉpu celuttukiṉṟāṉ; ippoḻutu appaṟṟu karuttup poruḷ paṟṟāka amaintu viṭukiṉṟatu. acciṟuvaṉ tuṇivāṉa oruvarai virumpalām; piṟaku tuṇivāṉavarkaḷ aṉaivaraiyum virumpuvāṉ; iṟutiyākat tuṇivu eṉpataiyē virumpum nilai avaṉiṭam amaintu viṭukiṉṟatu.
aṟap paṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam amaikiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai āciriyarkaḷ naṉku uṇarntu ataṟkēṟpak kuḻantai vāḻum cūḻnilaiyai naṟpaṇpukaḷiṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷāl nirappa vēṇṭum. kuḻantai iḷaṅkumarap paruvattai eytiyatum ilakkiyattilum varalāṟṟilum kāṇum kuṟikkōḷ māntar kaḷai avaṉukku ilakkāka vaikkalām. irāmaṉ, parataṉ, ilakkuvaṉ pōṉṟa kāviya māntarkaḷaiyum, periya purāṇattil kāṇum pala nāyaṉmārkaḷaiyum iṉṉum ivarkaḷ pōṉṟa palaraiyum kuṟikkōḷ māntarkaḷākak karutalām. puttaruṭaiya vāḻkkai ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāṉa vāḻkkaiyākum. nammuṭaiya nāṭṭut tantai kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyum ittakaiyatē.
ittakaiya aṟap paṟṟukkaḷaip peṟṟu, avaṟṟai naṉku cintittu, avaṟṟait taṟpaṟṟiṉuḷ7 aṭakki yamaippatē aṟattuṟaip paṇpāṭeṉpatu. naṉmai, uṇmai, tūymai ākiyavaṟṟai nām virumpupavarkaḷāka nammai nām karutiṉāl, ippaṇpukaḷ nammuṭaiya aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil talai kāṭṭum. ivaṟṟai ārāyuṅkāl aṭai nilaikkum avā nilaikkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumaiyai niṉaivukūrtal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. uṇmaiyil, eṉṉiṭamuḷḷa ellāp paṟṟukkaḷum eṉṉuṭaiya taṟpaṟṟaic cuṟṟilum amaikiṉṟaṉa. eṉṉiṭam uṇmaiyil uṟutiyāka illāta, āṉāl nāṉ avāvi niṟkum, paṟṟukkaḷ yāvai? ittakaiya paṟṟukkaḷtām kuṟikkōḷkaḷ8; ivaṟṟait taḻuvik koṇṭu niṟkum tāṉ eṉpatai nām karutalām. nām evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu intat tāṉ eṉpatai virumpu kiṉṟōmō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu ataṉai aṭaital kūṭum. atait teḷivākak karuti ataṉai aṭaiya muyaluvōmāyiṉ, nām mika uyarnta oḻukkanilaikku uyarntu koṇṭirukkiṉṟōm eṉṟa nilai ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.
ivviṭattil oṉṟu niṉaivu kūrtaṟpālatu. pala paṟṟukkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu oḻuṅku peṟuṅkāl, oru paṟṟu ciṟantatākavum ēṉaiyavai tāḻntavaikaḷākavum amaiyum. icciṟanta paṟṟai mutaṉmaip paṟṟu9 eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. ittalaimaippaṟṟē oruvaruṭaiya vāḻkkaiyiṉ mukkiya nōkkattaik kuṟikkum. ippaṟṟutāṉ ēṉaiya paṟṟukkaḷai āṭkoḷḷukiṉṟatu. ēṉaiya paṟṟukkaḷ taṉittaṉiyāka iyaṅkuvataiviṭa avai ippaṟṟiṉkīḻ iyaṅkum poḻututāṉ ōr uyiriyiṉ naṭattaiyil orumaippāṭu amaikiṉṟatu. mēlum, piṟa poruḷkaḷaic cuṟṟip paṟṟukkaḷ uṇṭāvatupōl nammaik kuṟittum paṟṟu ēṟpaṭum. itait taṉ-matippuppaṟṟu10 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar uḷaviyalār. itait tāṉ ulakavaḻakkil taṉ māṉam11 eṉṟu collukiṉṟōm. itil nammaic cuṟṟi uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ amaikiṉṟaṉa. maṉitaṉ taṉṉuṭaiya taṉmaiyait taṉip poruḷākavum matippuṭaiyatākavum karutukiṉṟāṉ; pala paṭṭaṟivukaḷaip peṟukiṉṟāṉ; taṉ-matippai vaḷarkka muyalukiṉṟāṉ. itaṉ mūlamākavē uḷḷatteḻum muraṇpāṭukaḷait tīrttal iyalum; kāntiyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟa periyārkaḷiṭam taṉ-matippuppaṟṟu ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu; avarkaḷ taṉmaiyil ōr orumaippāṭṭaiyum kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu.
paṟṟukkaḷiṉ vakai : paṟṟukkaḷ iruvakaippaṭum. oṉṟu, uṭaṉpāṭṭuppaṟṟu; maṟṟoṉṟu, etirmaṟaippaṟṟu. aḵtāvatu, nammiṭam viruppappaṟṟum12 ēṟpaṭalām; veṟuppup paṟṟum13 uṇṭākalām. cila eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ itaṉait teḷivākkum. nāṭṭuppaṟṟu eṉṟa iyalpūkkam piṟa nāṭukaḷai veṟuppataṉālum amaiyalām; puraṭci eṉṟa paṟṟu kuṟikkōḷ nāṭṭaik kāṇavēṇṭum eṉṟa avāviṉālum amaiyalām; allatu aṉṟaiya araciṉmītu koṇṭuḷḷa veṟuppiṉālum uṇṭākalām. uṇmai eṉṟa paṟṟu uṇmai eṉṟa paṇpiṉ mītu uḷḷa ārvattiṉālum ēṟpaṭalām; allatu poymaiyiṉ mītuḷḷa veṟuppiṉālum uṇṭākalām. māṇākkarkaḷiṭam uṭaṉpāṭṭup paṟṟukkaḷ amaivatē virumpattakkatu. ciṟanta āḷumai vaḷarccikku ivaikaḷē aṭippaṭai yāṉavai. etirmaṟaip paṟṟukkaḷ neṟikōṇiya vāḻvil koṇṭu celuttiviṭum.
paṟṟukkaḷ uṇṭākum muṟai : paṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai ārāyvōm. oru kuḻantaiyiṉ vāḻkkai, toṭakkattil iyalpūkkattil mītūrntu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivai ceyaṟpaṭuvatil yātoru oḻuṅkum illai. kuḻantaiyiṉ cūḻnilai ataṟkup putiya nilaimaikaḷait tantu koṇṭē irukkiṉṟatu. kuḻantaiyiṉ iyalpūkkaṅkaḷ avvappoḻutu eḻum viḻaivukaḷukkum tēvaikaḷukkum ēṟṟavāṟē ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷaṭaivil taṉittaṉiyāka, tōṉṟiyapaṭiyellām ceyaṟpaṭṭa iyalpūkkaṅkaḷ oru kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉuḷ aṭaṅki oru kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷaic cuṟṟi iyaṅkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. paṭṭaṟivu vaḷara vaḷara, kāṭcip paṟṟu, karuttuppaṟṟu, aṟappaṟṟu, mutaṉmaippaṟṟu, taṉ-matippup paṟṟu pōṉṟavaikaḷākat tulakkamuṟukiṉṟaṉa.
oru kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷaic cuṟṟi orupaṟṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiṉāl, apporuḷaippaṟṟi iyaṅkum attaṉiyāḷiṉ iyalpūkkaṅkaḷ appaṟṟiṉāl kaṭṭuppaṭuttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. apporuḷuṭaṉ icaintupōkum iyalpūkkaṅkaḷ valup peṟukiṉṟaṉa; apporuḷuṭaṉ muraṇpaṭṭu niṟkum iyal pūkkaṅkaḷ kaṭṭuppaṭuttap peṟṟu maṭṭuppaṭuttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. toṭakkattil ciṟuvaṉ aṉṉaiyiṭam aḷavaṟṟa aṉpu kāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ uṇavūṭṭum poḻutum cīrāṭṭum poḻutum iṉpuṟukiṉṟāṉ. avaḷait taḻuvukiṉṟāṉ; avaḷuṭaṉ koñcukiṉṟāṉ; viḷaiyāṭukiṉṟāṉ; ivvāṟu pala iṉpuṟuttum ceyalkaḷaip purikiṉṟāṉ. avaḷ avaṉaik kaṭintu koṇṭāl, avaḷuṭaṉ ciṉaṅkoḷḷukiṉṟāṉ; avaḷuṭaṉ pēca maṟukkiṉṟāṉ; allatu avaḷait tiṭṭu kiṉṟāṉ. cila camayam ciṉam mītūrntu niṟkum poḻutu avaḷuṭaiya āṭaiyaik kaṭittukkiḻikkiṉṟāṉ. ittakaiya ceyalkaḷ tiṭīreṉat tōṉṟupavai; toṭarpaṟṟavai. āṉāl, caṟṟu vaḷarnta piṟaku avaṉuṭaiya naṭattaiyum māṟukiṉṟatu. nāḷaṭaivil taṉ pēcciṉālum, naṭattai yiṉālum avaḷ maṉattaip puṇpaṭutta virumpuvatillai; taṉtāyiṉ nalattaip poruṭpaṭuttat toṭaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. ippoḻutu avaḷuṭaiya kuṟaikaḷ yāvum niṟaikaḷākap pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tāṉ ceyyum ovvoru ceyalum avaḷai evvāṟu pātikkum eṉṟu matippiṭukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ taṉ aṉṉaiyiṭam koṇṭuḷḷa aṉpup paṟṟu avaṉuṭaiya vāḻvilum naṭattaiyilum ōr orumaippāṭu, oḻuṅku, nilaipput taṉmai, toṭarcci mutaliyavaṟṟai uṇṭākkiviṭukiṉṟatu.
taṉippaṭṭa oru paṟṟu vaḷarntu iyalpūkka vāḻkkaiyai oḻuṅkum icaivum kūṭiyatākac ceyvataip pōlavē, paṟṟukkaḷ yāvum ōr oḻuṅkil amaintu oṉṟōṭoṉṟu iṇainta amaippāka vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. tāḻnta paṟṟukkaḷ uyarnta paṟṟukkaḷākavum, uyarnta paṟṟukkaḷ mutaṉmaip paṟṟākavum vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. nāṭṭuppaṟṟu evvāṟu vaḷarkiṉṟatu eṉpatai eṭuttukkāṭṭākak koṇṭu itaṉaiviḷakkuvōm. ciṟuvaṉ mutalil peṟṟōriṭam paṟṟu koḷkiṉṟāṉ; ippaṟṟu nāḷaṭaivil viḷaiyāṭṭut tōḻarkaḷ, piṟa kuṭumpaṅkaḷ, āciriyarkaḷ, paḷḷikaḷ, māvaṭṭam pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ pāl muṟaiyē virintucelkiṉṟatu. kuṭumpap paṟṟu mutaliyavai muṟaiyē ovvoṉṟukkum tāḻntavaiyāka amaintu nāṭṭup paṟṟukku mutaṉmai koṭuttaṉa. nāṭṭuppaṟṟum tāḻnta paṟṟāka amaintu māṉiṭa iṉappaṟṟai mutaṉmaiyuṭaiyatāka oppuk koḷḷalām. māṉiṭa iṉattiṉ nalattai nammuṭaiya vāḻkkaiyiṉ payaṉākak koṇṭāl, nammuṭaiya taṉ-matippup paṟṟut talaiyōṅki ēṉaiya paṟṟukkaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum aṭakkiyāḷak kūṭiyatāka amaiyum. itaṉ vaḷarcciyē namatu oḻukkattiṉ vaḷarcci eṉṟu kūṟalām. eṉavē, oḻukka vaḷarcciyil uḷḷak kiḷarcci nilai, kāṭcip poruḷ paṟṟunilai, aṟappaṟṟu nilai, taṉ-matippup paṟṟu nilai eṉṟa nāṉku paṭikaḷaik kāṇkiṉṟōm.
paṟṟukkaḷum naṭattaiyum : paṟṟukkaḷē naṭattaiyai aṟutiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai naṭattaiyiṉ ovvoru pākattilum piratipalikkiṉṟaṉa. kālaiyil ceytittāḷkaḷ paṭippataṟku varukiṉṟaṉar. palar avaṟṟaip paṭikkiṉṟaṉar. oruvar uḷnāṭṭu araciyal ceytikaḷaiyum maṟṟoruvar viḷaiyāṭṭup pakuti kaḷaiyum, piṟitoruvar viḷamparap pakutikaḷaiyum, iṉṉoruvar veḷināṭṭu araciyal ceytikaḷaiyum ūkkamākap paṭippataik kāṇalām. itaṉāl avaravaruṭaiya vāḻkkaip paṟṟukaḷ iṉṉavai eṉpatai aṟiyalām.
eṉavē, oruvaruṭaiya paṟṟukkaḷai aṟintāl, avaruṭaiya naṭattai eppaṭiyirukkum eṉpatai muṉṉarē uraittu viṭalām. ākavē, nām aṭikkaṭi paḻakum maṉitarkaḷiṉ paṟṟukkaḷai aṟiyavēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamai yātatākiṉṟatu; avaruṭaiya naṭattaiyē uṟṟu nōkkutal mūlantāṉ ivaṟṟai aṟiyalām.
paṟṟukkaḷ naṭattaiyai ōraḷavu orumaippāṭaṭaiyac ceyyumāyiṉum, uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷaip pōlavē ivaiyum naṭattaiyaip pala tikkukaḷil koṇṭu celuttum. ip paṟṟukkaḷai aṭakkiyāḷa oru talaimaippaṟṟu iṉṟiya maiyātatu. ivaṟṟaik kīḻē kāṇpōm.
ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ naṭattaiyil paṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam paṅku koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataic caṟṟu ārāyvōm. ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉ tantaiyiṉ aṟivaik kaṇṭu viyakkiṉṟāṉ. piṟaku ācāṉatu aṟivu avaṉukku viyappiṉai viḷaivikkiṉṟatu. aṟivuḷḷa ivarkaḷiṭam avaṉukku aṉpu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. piṟaku talaimaiyāciriyariṉ aṟivu, paḷḷiyilpalanikaḻccikaḷil paṅku koḷḷum periyārkaḷiṉ aṟivu avaṉ uḷḷattaik kavarkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷiṭam paṟṟu ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. nāḷaṭaivil ippaṟṟu aṟivu paṟṟiya paṟṟāka māṟukiṉṟatu. aṟivaip peṟuvataiyē mutaṉmaip paṟṟākavum avaṉiṭam amaiyalām; aḵtē avaṉuṭaiya taṉ-matippup paṟṟākavum vaṭivu peṟalām. aṟivuṭaiyār ellām uṭaiyār eṉṟa vaḷḷuvap perun takaiyiṉ vākku itaṉai uṟutippaṭuttavum ceyyalām. ciṟanta aṟiviṉaip peṟuvataiyē avaṉ vāḻkkaip payaṉākap-kuṟikkōḷākak-karutip piṟavaṟṟait tāḻntavaiyāka eṇṇalām. aṟivaip paṟṟiya paṟṟu ivvāṟu tōṉṟum; aṟivut toṭarpuḷḷa ceytikaḷil viyappu aṭaivāṉ; taṉ aṟivai veḷiyiṭuṅkāl taṉṉeṭuppum, taṉṉiṉ mikka aṟivuṭaiyāraik kāṇuṅkāl taṉṉoṭukkamum avaṉiṭam kāṇappeṟum. ciṟanta nūlkaḷaip paṭippatilum, avaṟṟait tiraṭṭuvatilum avaṉiṭam ārvam talaikāṭṭum. iṅṅaṉam avaṉuṭaiya vaṉmaiyāṉa uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ yāvum aṟivaiyoṭṭiyē kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa; orumaippāṭum aṭaikiṉṟaṉa. ēṉaiya paṟṟukkaḷaiviṭa aṟivuppaṟṟē ivaṉiṭam mītūrntu niṟkum; ituvē vāḻkkaiyil mukkiyamāṉatākavum tōṉṟum.
iṉṉoruvaṉ ariccantiraṉaippōl cattiyattaiyē kuṟikkōḷ poruḷākak koḷḷalām. piṟitoruvaṉukkuk kāviyat talaivarkaḷ kuṟikkōḷ poruḷkaḷāka amaiyalām. tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattil varum aṭiyārkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai cilarukku ilaṭciyamāka amaiyalām. eṉavē, vāḻkkaiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷ oruvaruṭaiya naṭattaiyait tīrmāṉikkiṉṟatu. ataṉ poruṭṭu etaiyum tuṟappataṟku āyattamāka iruppār. kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ vāḻkkai itaṟku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāka amaiyum.
taṉ-matippup paṟṟu : taṉ-matippup paṟṟu eṉpatai mēlē kuṟippiṭṭōm aṉṟō? aḵtu eṅṅaṉam vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟatu eṉpataic ciṟitu ārāyvōm. ciṟuvaṉ iḷamaiyilēyē taṉṉaic cūḻnilaiyiliruntu pirittu aṟikiṉṟāṉ. veḷiyulakuṭaṉ toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟap peṟāta avaṉ aṉupavak kūṟukaḷ yāvum tiṟaṉ eṉṟa uṭkaruvāka (rēuṭanaraḷa) amai kiṉṟatu. itil moḻi avaṉukkut tuṇaiyāka niṟkiṉṟatu; avaṉuṭaiya ciṟappup peyar(iṭukuṟippeyar) avaṉukku oru kaippiṭiyāka amaiya, ataṉaik koṇṭu piṟavaṟṟait taṉṉiliruntu pirittaṟikiṉṟāṉ. tāṉ uyiri eṉṟum, poruḷkaḷ uyirili eṉṟum, tāṉ etaic ceyya iyalum, etaic ceyya iyalātu eṉpataiyum pākupaṭutti uṇarkiṉṟāṉ. nāḷaṭaivil taṉṉuṭaiya naṭattaiyaiyē tiṟaṉāyum āṟṟalaiyum peṟukiṉṟāṉ. taṉ tōḻarkaḷ taṉṉaip pukaḻvataiyum ikaḻvataiyum kāṇkiṉṟāṉ; iccamūka aṟiviṉāl taṉṉai iraṇṭu nilaikaḷil aṟikiṉṟāṉ. tāṉ cintaṉaiyiṉ ilakkākavum, cintaṉaiyāḷaṉākavum iruppatai uṇarkiṉṟāṉ. paṭippaṭiyākat taṉṉuṇarcci14 vaḷarat toṭaṅku kiṉṟatu. itiliruntu tāṉ taṉmatippup paṟṟu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. itaic cuṟṟit taṉṉeṭuppu, taṉṉoṭukkam, accam, ciṉampōṉṟa uḷḷak kiḷarccikaḷ amaikiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu taṉṉai oru cila viruppu veṟuppukkaḷ, eṇṇaṅkaḷ, karuttukkaḷ, āṟṟalkaḷ, tiṟaṉkaḷ, cuvaikaḷ, vāḻkkaik kōlaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ nilaikkaḷaṉākak karutukiṉṟāṉ. taṉṉaip paṟṟiya tiṭṭamāṉa karuttu avaṉiṭam amaikiṉṟatu; tāṉ `ippaṭippaṭṭa oḻukka muṭaiyavaṉeṉṟum karutukiṉṟāṉ. taṉṉuṭaiya tōḻarkaḷ taṉṉai naṭattum muṟai taṉ nilaiyai aṟivataṟkup payaṉ paṭuvatōṭaṉṟi, tāṉ eppaṭi irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatai aṟiyavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. itu kalvittuṟaikku oruciṟanta vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amaikiṉṟatu. ciṟuvarkaḷ taṅkaḷaippaṟṟic ciṟanta eṇṇaṅkaḷaik koḷḷum paṭi peṟṟōrkaḷum āciriyarkaḷum vaḻivakai ceyyavēṇṭum. iḵtu avarkaḷiṉ mutaṟkaṭamai yēyaṉṟi mutalāya kaṭamaiyu mākum. tammaip piṟar nērmaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ, uṇmai yuḷḷavarkaḷ, nampikkai vaikkat takkavarkaḷ, uḻaippāḷikaḷ eṉṟu karutiṉāl avarkaḷum avvaruṅ kuṇaṅkaḷukkup pāttirarkaḷāvar; tammai appaṇpuṭaivarkaḷākavē karuti aṅṅaṉamē naṭantu koḷvar. avarkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷil ētēṉum kuṟai iruppataic cuṭṭiyuraittāl avarkaḷ nāṇamuṟṟu ataṉai nīkka muyaluvar. aṅṅaṉamiṉṟi piṟar tammait tīyavarkaḷ, payaṉaṟṟavarkaḷ, nampikkai yaṟṟavarkaḷ, cōmparkaḷ eṉak karutiṉāl, avarkaḷum tam koṭumaiyai uṟuti ceytukoḷvar; attakaiya ceyalkaḷil cerukkum koḷvar. eṉavē, cariyāṉa matippaic ciṟuvarkaḷiṭam amaiyac ceyvatu kalviyāḷarkaḷiṉ kaṭamaiyākum. “nī aṅṅaṉam ceyvāy eṉṟu nāṉ karutavillai”, “nī puriyum ceyalā atu?”, orukaṇita āciriyariṉ makaṉā inta matippeṇ peṟuvatu? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa tūṇṭuraikaḷ ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ taṉmatippaic ciṟanta muṟaiyil vaḷarkkat tuṇai ceyyum; ittakaiya pukaḻuraikaḷ avarkaḷiṉ ceyalkaṭku orumaippāṭu tarum; uyarnta camūkattil ciṟanta uṟuppiṉarkaḷākac ceyyum. avai avarkaḷuṭaiya taṉ matippu, taṉmāṉam, oḻukkam ākiyavaṟṟai vaḷarkkum. mutalil avarkaḷ kuṟik kōḷaiyoṭṭi vāḻa muyaluvar; mutalil `kuṟikkōḷ-tāṉ (ṉainayaṭa-ḷanaṭaka) eṉpataṟku matipput tantu piṟaku atu piṟappikkum āṇaikaḷukkum kīḻppaṭintu naṭappar. iṅṅaṉam taṉ matippup paṟṟu vaḷarcciyuṟṟu vāḻkkaiyil orumaip pāṭum nilaip puttaṉmaiyum amaiyak kāraṇamākiṉṟatu. ittakaiya taṉ-matippai vaḷarkkum poruṭṭē taṟkālattil iḷaṅkuṟṟa vāḷikaḷ15 ciṟaiccālaikku aṉuppappeṟāmal tiruttac cālaikku aṉuppap peṟukiṉṟaṉar.
oruvaruṭaiya viruppu veṟuppukkaḷ niṟaivēṟiṉāl kaḷippu16 uṇṭākiṉṟatu; avai niṟaivēṟāviṭiṉ aruvaruppu17k koḷḷac ceykiṉṟatu. cila camayam ivvaruvaruppu vāḻk kaiyilēyē veṟupput taṭṭum aḷavukkukkūṭa vaḷarntu viṭukiṉṟatu. mutalil taṉ-matippup paṟṟu kuṭumpattaiyoṭṭi amaikiṉṟatu; nammuṭaiya viruppu muḻuvatum inta vaṭṭat tilēyē cuḻaluvatāl, aṅku nām kaḷippaṭaikiṉṟōm; allatu ūkkamiḻantu niṟkiṉṟōm. nāḷaṭaivil ivvaṭṭam virivaṭaikiṉṟatu; taṉṉaiyottavarkaḷiṭaiyē paḷḷic ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉeṭuppum taṉṉoṭukkamum18 koḷkiṉṟāṉ. iṅṅaṉam vāḻkkai muḻuvatum oppāriṉ19 ēṟpaiyē (matippu) avāvi niṟkiṉṟōm.
avamatippum āṉṟa matippum iraṇṭu
mikaimakka ḷāṉmatikkaṟ pāla20 
eṉṟa nālaṭiyārai nōkkuka. iṅṅaṉamē, oru nūlāciriyaṉum taraṅkuṟaintavarkaḷiṉ pukaḻuraikaḷaiyō ikaḻuraikaḷaiyō poruṭpaṭuttu villai.
paṭippaṭiyāka nammuṭaiya naṭattaiyiṉ ēṟpiṟkō, aṉṟi ēṟpiṉmaikkō nām intak kuṟikkōḷ-taṉaiyē nōkku kiṉṟōm. muṉṉark kuṟippiṭṭa aṟappaṟṟukkaḷ vaḷarcci yuṟṟavuṭaṉ, taṉiyāḷ kuṟikkōḷ camūkattiṉ uṟuppiṉaṉāki viṭukiṉṟāṉ. nammuṭaiya tāṉ uṟuppāka ilaṅkum antac camūkattiṟkē nām atika matippiṉait tarukiṉṟōm. innilai aṭaintatum, nām taṉ-vaḷarcciyiṉ21 ellai kaḷaiyē neruṅki viṭukiṉṟōm. ippoḻutu ellā iyalpūkkaṅkaḷum kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉuḷ aṭaṅki kuṟikkōḷ-tāṉāl neṟippaṭuttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaittāṉ nām taṉ-aṭakkam22 allatu taṉ kaṭṭuppāṭu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm. ittakaiya taṉ-aṭakkam nīṇṭa nāḷ payiṟcikkup piṟakē eytak kūṭiyatu; intap payiṟciyiṉ poḻutu kuṟikkōḷ-tāṉ vaḷarcci peṟṟut tulakkamuṟukiṉṟatu. perumpālōriṭam innilai avāvap peṟukiṉṟatu; āṉāl, atai eytutal aritākavēyuḷḷatu.
oḻukkamum maṉauṟutiyum23
iyalpūkkac ceyal mukkūṟukaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. poruḷkaḷai aṟikiṉṟōm; itu aṟivu nilai24 poruḷkaḷai aṟiyumpoḻutu nām vēṇṭiya poruḷāyiṉ kaḷikkiṉṟōm; vēṇṭāp poruḷāyiṉ veṟukkiṉṟōm. ivvāṟu viruppum veṟuppum kalantatu uṇarccinilai.25 nām vēṇṭum poruḷāyiṉ ataṉai aṭaiya nam maṉam patai pataikkiṉṟatu; tuṭituṭikkiṉṟatu. innilai iyaṟṟinilai26 eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟum. ivai taṉittaṉiyē iyaṅkā; avai āḷumaiyiṉ maṉa vaṉmaikaḷum alla. oru maṉitaṉ eṇṇumpoḻutu, uṇarum poḻutu allatu ceyalāṟṟumpoḻututāṉ āḷumai27 muḻutum ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, maṉauṟuti eṉpatu maṉavaṉmai28 aṉṟu eṉpatākiṉṟatu. atu āḷumaiyiṉ oru kuṟippiṭṭa ceyalaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. oru maṉitaṉ aṉupavaṅ kaḷaiyum uḷḷuṇarvaiyum29 taḻuvi ataṉpaṭi naṭattalē maṉa uṟutiyākum. maṉa uṟutiyaittāṉ tirukkuṟaḷ maṉattiṇmai eṉakkuṟik kiṉṟatu. oruvaruṭaiya tāṉ muḻuvatum tīrmāṉam ceytu ataṉpaṭi ceyalāṟṟukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, maṉa uṟuti eṉpatu āḷumaiyiṉ ceyaṟpaṭu nilaiyākum. mākkēl eṉṟa uḷaviyal aṟiñar ceyaṟpaṭum oḻukkamē maṉa uṟuti eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
paṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu mēlē kaṇṭōmallavā? paṟṟukkaḷil nallaṉavum uṇṭu; tīyaṉavum uṇṭu. ippaṟṟukkaḷ mika vaṉmaiyāka iruppiṉ taṉiyāḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyil nirantaramāṉa iyalpukaḷākap paṭintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila camayam paṟṟukkaḷiṭaiyē pōrāṭṭam30 nikaḻtal uṇṭu. cila eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ itaṉait teḷivākkum. nāḷai naṭaipeṟa irukkum tērvukkup paṭippatā, allatu iṉṟiravu naṭaipeṟavirukkum arumaiyāṉa paṭakkāṭcikkuppōvatā? eṉṟu māṇākkaṉ eṇṇukiṉṟāṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ āciriyar vēlaikkup payiṟci peṟukiṉṟāṉ; payiṟci iṉṉum oṉṟiraṇṭu tiṅkaḷil muṭintuviṭum. appoḻutu eḻuttar vēlai31 niyamaṉa āṇai oṉṟu avaṉai vantaṭaikiṉṟatu. payiṟciyai muṭippatā, aṉṟi eḻuttar pataviyai ēṟṟukkoḷvatā? eṉṟu kalaṅki niṟkiṉṟāṉ. iṉṉoru iḷaiñaṉ, kātalūkkattai niṟaivēṟṟa illattiliruppatā, allatu camūkat toṇṭu puriya veḷic celvatā? eṉṟu cintaṉai ceykiṉṟāṉ. iraṇṭilumuḷḷa naṉmai tīmaikaḷai ārāyntu ētāvatoṉṟait tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum. iḵtu avaṉuṭaiya paṟṟukkaḷaip poṟuttatākum. ittakaiya iṭarppāṭāṉa nilaikaḷil tāṉ mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa taṉ matippuppaṟṟu tuṇaiyāka niṟkiṉṟatu. aḵtu avaṉukku vēṇṭiya āṟṟalait tantu piṟavaḻikaḷaip puṟakkaṇittu aṭakkiyāḷac ceyyum. aḵtāvatu, tāḻnta uḷ-tuṭippukkaḷaiyum iyalpūkkaṅkaḷaiyum kuṟikkōḷāka niṟkum taṉ matippuppaṟṟiṉ āṟṟalāl vellutal ākum.
maṉa uṟutiyiṉ uram iyalpūkkaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil iyaṅkum uḷtuṭippukkaḷiṉ urattaip poṟuttatu. maṉa uṟutiyiṉ taram avaṉ koṇṭa kuṟikkōḷiṉ tarattaiyum, pōrāṭum kuṟikkōḷka ḷiṉmaiyai yum poṟuttatu. kuṟikkōḷ ciṟappāka amaiyiṉ maṉa uṟutiyum ciṟantatāka amaiyum; oḻukkamum uyarntatāka amaiyum. eṉavē, māṇākkarkaḷiṭam cariyāṉa maṉauṟuti amaiya āciriyarkaḷum peṟṟōrkaḷum takka kavaṉam celuttit takunta nilaiyil payiṟciyum aḷittal vēṇṭum.
māṇākkarkaḷiṭam maṉa uṟutiyai vaḷarkka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, paḷḷi kaḷil avarkaḷ tāmākac ceyalāṟṟavum poṟup pēṟkavum vāyppukkaḷai nalkutal vēṇṭum. taṉṉāṭci muṟai, taṉivēlai, kuṭimaip payiṟci pōṉṟa tuṟaikaḷil āciriyar mēṟkoḷḷum muyaṟcikaḷ itaṟkup peritum payaṉpaṭum. aṉupavamiṉmaiyāl māṇākkarkaḷ tavaṟu iḻaittal kūṭum eṉa eṇṇi avarkaḷ ceyyavēṇṭiya muṭivukaḷai nāmē avarkaḷukkāka iyaṟṟuvatil avarkaḷukku nalampayakkātu eṉpatai nām uṇarntu varukiṉṟōm. uramāṉa uṟutiyuḷḷa peṟṟōriṉ kuḻantaikaḷil palar uramaṟṟa maṉa uṟuti yuḷḷavarkaḷāka iruppataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? peṟṟōr tam piḷḷaikaḷukku maṉauṟutiyil payiṟciyaḷikkāmaiyē. ciṟuvayati liruntē tavaṟiḻaikkātu taṭukkappeṟṟa oruvaṉ vaḷarntavaṉāṉa piṟaku paranta ivvulakil taṉ aluvalkaḷait tiṟamaiyuṭaṉ mēṟkoḷvāṉ eṉṟu karutuvatu payaṉaṟṟa ceyalē. ceyal tavaṟākap pōkumeṉṟu añci cariyāṉataiyum iyaṟṟat tuṇivu illātavarai uṇṭākkum kalvi muṟaiyāl yātu payaṉ? itu kalvi taṉ kaṭamaiyil tavaṟiyatāka muṭiyumallavā? cila camayam payiṟci yaḷikkum aḷavu aḷavu mīṟavum kūṭum. aḷavukkumīṟiṉāl amirtamum nañcu aṉṟō? aḷavukku mīṟiṉāl, piṭivātamum, muraṭṭut taṉamumē viḷaivukaḷāka vantu cērum. āyiṉum, neṟikkalviyil32 tuṇitalukkup payiṟci taruvataiyum cērkkavēṇṭum. oḻukkap payiṟciyil aṟivu nilaik kūṟukkum uṇarcci nilaik kūṟukkum takka kavaṉam celuttap peṟṟāl, tuṇitaliṉ payiṟci aṟivuṭai maiyākavum irukkum; naṉmaiyāṉatākavum amaiyum.
kuṟikkōḷkaḷum vāḻkkait tattuva vaḷarcciyum
muḻut taṉmai peṟṟa oḻukkattil cila kuṟikkōḷkaḷ niccayam ātikkam celuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai nām uṟutiyākak kūṟalām.
	oḻukkam viḻuppam taralāṉ oḻukkam 
	uyiriṉum ōmpap paṭum. 33
eṉṟa kuṟaḷ kāṭṭum oḻukkam ittakaiyatē. camūkam pārāṭṭum allatu paḻittuk kūṟum naṭattaiyaiviṭak kuṟikkōḷkaḷ neṟippaṭuttum naṭattaiyē mika uyarntatu. iṅṅaṉam tammiṭa muḷḷa uḷḷoḷi yāl iyaṅkuvōr oru cilarē; camūkat tākkutalkaḷai avarkaḷ ciṟitum poruṭpaṭuttamāṭṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ irukkum iṭam teriyāmal maṟaintu kiṭappar. ittakaiya kuṟikkōḷkaḷ kuḻantaiyiṉ vāḻkkaiyil mika metuvāka ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa; avai perumpālum kuḻantai atikamāka matikkum allatu virumpum makkaḷiṭamiruntē - (e-ṭu) peṟṟōrkaḷ, āciriyarkaḷ, naṇparkaḷ - karuttēṟṟattiṉ34 mūlam amaikiṉṟaṉa. aṟappaṟṟukkaḷ eṅṅaṉam vaḷarukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai mēlē uraittōm. atai iṉṉum caṟṟu viḷakku vōm. mākkēl eṉṟa uḷaviyal aṟiñar mukkiyamākat toṟṟunōy pōṉṟa ottuṇarcciyālum, ciṟanta periyōr kaḷiṉ karuttēṟṟattālum avai uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ vīra-vaḻipāṭṭālum35 ivai ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. perumpālum vāḻvil inta uyarnta camūka vaḷarcciyaip peṟuvataṟku vāyppukkaḷ amaivatillai. ciṟanta aruṅ kuṇaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa uyarnta talaivarkaḷai aṭikkaṭi cantittu avarkaḷuṭaṉ paḻakum vāyppukkaḷum ēṟpaṭu vatillai. aṉṟiyum, avarkaḷ paḻakum talaivarkaḷiṭamum pala kuṟaikaḷ kuṉṟiṉmēl viḷakkupōl veḷippaṭaiyākat terikiṉṟaṉa. cilar nūlkaḷiṉ mūlam uyirppaiyum uṟcākat taiyum peṟukiṉṟaṉar; āṉāl, nēr muṟaiyil ciṟanta periyōrkaḷiṉ vāyilākap peṟum kuṟikkōḷkaḷē niṟainta palaṉkaḷai viḷaivikkum. oru kuṟikkōḷ mītu nām koḷḷum ārvattiṟkēṟpa ataṉai aṭaiya muṟpaṭuvōm. nām mēṟ koḷḷum kuṟikkōḷaippaṟṟit teḷivāṉa karuttuṭaṉ pala iṭarppāṭukaḷaiyum poruṭpaṭuttātu avaṟṟai etirttuk kuṟikkōḷai nōkki muṉṉēṟiṉāl, nal vāḻkkaiyiṉ cikarattai eṭṭip piṭikkalām.
kuṟikkōḷ vaḷarcciyāl uṇṭākum vāḻkkait tattuvam nam maṉattaiyum ceyalaiyum neṟippaṭuttukiṉṟatu. vāḻkkait tattuvam eṉpatu ōr arumporuḷāka iruppiṉum, nām ellōrum aṟintō aṟiyāmalō ataṉai amaittuk koṇṭuḷḷōm. nām mēṟ koṇṭuḷḷa kuṟikkōḷ kaḷukkēṟpa, vāḻkkaiyil palavaṟṟait tērnteṭukkiṉṟōm. kuṇam nāṭik kuṟṟam nāṭi, avaṟṟuḷ mikai nāṭi mikka koḷkiṉṟōm. kuṇamum kuṟṟamum avaravar koṇṭuḷḷa kuṟikkōḷkaḷaip poṟuttavai. ciṟanta kalviyiṉ nōkkam eṉṉa eṉpatai vaḷḷuvap peruntakai,
	kaṟṟataṉāl āya payaṉeṉkol? vālaṟivaṉ
	naṟṟāḷ toḻāar eṉiṉ.36
eṉṟu kūṟuvar. aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭaṭaital nūṟpayaṉē37 eṉa ilakkaṇa nūl kūṟum. ivaiyē namatu taṉ-matippup paṟṟāka amaital vēṇṭum. inta uyarnta kuṟik kōḷkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci kuṭumpam, paḷḷi, camūkam eṉṟa cūḻ nilaikaḷaip poṟuttatu. 
oḻukkat tulakkattil paḷḷiyiṉ paṅku
kuḻantaiyiṉ vaḷarcci kuṭumpattil toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, kuṭumpattiṉ poṟuppu avvaḷarcciyil atikamākiṉṟatu. eṉṟālum, paḷḷikaṭkum ittuṟaiyil ciṟanta poṟuppu irukkattāṉ ceykiṉṟatu. uyarnta kuṟikkōḷkaḷaiyum, taṉ-matippup paṟṟaiyum maṉattiṇmaiyaiyum vaḷarppatil paḷḷikkuc ciṟanta paṅku uṇṭu; atai atu eṭuttukkoḷḷattāṉ vēṇṭum. paḷḷi ataṟkeṉavē ēṟpaṭuttap peṟṟa oru nilaiyamākum. ippaṇiyait tiṟampaṭac ceyya vēṇṭu meṉpataṟkākavē taṉip payiṟcipeṟṟa āciriyarkaḷ aṅku niyamikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉar.
taṉ-matippup paṟṟu camūkam ēṟpaṭuttum paṇpu eṉṟu mēlē kaṇṭōm. vaḻivaḻiyāka vantu koṇṭirukkum paḷḷiyiṉ marapu,38 ataṉ kūṭṭu vāḻkkai, paḷḷiyil ēṟpāṭu ceyyappeṟṟirukkum viḷaiyāṭṭu vakaikaḷ, payiṟṟummuṟai, paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷiṉ celvākku mutaliyavai māṇākkarkaḷiṭam taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyilum kūṭivāḻ muṟaiyilum payaṉpaṭum ciṟanta paṟṟukkaḷai vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉa; ivai takka muṟaiyil neṟippaṭuttap peṟṟut taṉ matippup paṟṟāka amaintāl ciṟanta muṟaiyil oḻukkam amaiyum.
payiṟṟum āciriyarkaḷum uṭaṉ payilum tōḻarkaḷum kuḻantaiyiṭam taṉ-matippai uṇṭākkupavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ etirpārkkummuṟaiyil tāṉ kuḻantai naṭantu koḷḷum. avaṉiṭam amaiyum kuṟikkōḷkaḷ payiṟṟum āciriyar kaḷālum, payilum pāṭaṅkaḷālum, uṭaṉiruntu pāṭaṅkēṭkum ēṉaiya tōḻarkaḷālumē ēṟpaṭum. āciriyariṉ pukaḻuraikaḷ, kaṭuñ coṟkaḷ, ceyalkaḷ, uṇarccikaḷ, aṟivu pōṉṟa vaikaḷum paricilkaḷ, taṇṭaṉaikaḷ pōṉṟa ēṟpāṭukaḷum avaṉiṭam paṟṟukkaḷai uṇṭākkum. ivaṟṟaic cariyāka uṇarāta āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷukkuk kuṟumpukkāraṉ, oḻuṅkaṟṟavaṉ, maṭaiyaṉ, pōkkiri eṉṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷai vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉar! itaṉāl palar ippaṭṭaṅkaḷai ēṟṟu avai uṟutiyākumāṟu naṭantu koḷkiṉṟaṉar! ippaṭip payaṉaṟṟup pōkum māṇākkar eṇṇiṟantōr. ivaṟṟai etirkkum oru cila tuṇivāṉa māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyarkaḷukkē paṭṭamaḷippu viḻā naṭatti viṭukiṉṟaṉar!
vakuppaṟaikaḷilum, viḷaiyāṭukaḷaṅkaḷilum, piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum māṇākkarkaḷiṭam taṉṉampikkaiyum taṉ-matippum vaḷara vāyppukkaḷ nalkutal vēṇṭum. maṉa uṟuti vaḷaravum, ciṟanta paḻakkaṅkaḷai vaḷarkkavum iṭam taravēṇṭum. ec ceyalkaḷ ceytālum avaṟṟiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷai māṇākkar aṟiyac ceytal vēṇṭum. nōkkaṅkaḷ aṭaiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu māṇākkarkaḷ tāmāka muyaṉṟāltāṉ maṉattiṇmai vaḷarum. contak kavarccikaḷiṉṟēl cuṟucuṟuppum viṭā muyaṟciyum tōṉṟā. māṇākkarkaḷmuṉ vaikkap peṟum nōkkaṅkaḷ viraivil aṭaiyac kūṭiyaṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum. ennavēlaiyilum nirppantamum palavantamum kūṭā; etaiyum viṭutalai yuṇarcciyuṭaṉ ceytāl tāṉ maṉat tiṇmai vaḷarum. tērvukkumuṉ māṇākkarkaḷāl ceyyap peṟum vēlai āṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil ceyyap peṟuvatillai eṉpatu nām aṟintatē. itiliruntu āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpikkum muṟaikaḷilum piṟavaṟṟilum pala paṭippiṉaikaḷai aṟintu koḷḷalām. pāṭaṅkaḷ campantamākap pala tiṭṭaṅkaḷai ēṟpaṭuttikkoṇṭu avaṟṟai niṟaivēṟṟa vāyppukkaḷ taralām. iṉṟaiya kuṭimaip payiṟciyiluḷḷa pala naṟkūṟukaḷaik koṇṭu ivaṟṟai eḷitil amaikkalām. ciṟiya ceyalkaḷilum periya ceyalkaḷilum poṟuppu māṇākkarkaḷuṭaiyatāka amaintāl aṉaittum cīrpaṭum.
varalāṟṟup pāṭaṅkaḷ, ilakkiyappāṭaṅkaḷ, aṟiviyala ṟiñarkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiliruntu pala aṟiñarkaḷ, oḻukka cīlarkaḷ, toṇṭarkaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ kuṟik kōḷkaḷaik koṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷ tamakku vēṇṭiya kuṟikkōḷkaḷai ēṟpaṭuttikkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, ataṟkēṟṟa pakutikaḷ pāṭaṅkaḷil amaiyumāṟu ceytal vēṇṭum. paḷḷi nūlakaṅkaḷilum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ pala nilaikaḷukkēṟpa avarkaḷē paṭittaṟintu koḷḷakkūṭiya ittakaiya varalāṟukaḷ aṭaṅkiya pala puttakaṅkaḷai vāṅki vaittu avaṟṟai avarkaḷē virumpippaṭikkum vāyppuk kaḷaiyum nalkutal vēṇṭum.
oḻukkak kalvi tarum nēraṅkaḷil uṇmaiyiṉ iṉṟiya maiyāmaiyai uṇarttavum, poyyiṉ virumpattakāmaiyai vaṟpuṟuttavum ciṟu coṟ poḻivukaḷ naṭattuvatāl payaṉillai. ivai naṉku amaivataṟkup paḷḷi vāḻkkaiyilēyē pala vāyppukkaḷai nalki avaṟṟiṉ mūlam payiṟci yaḷittal vēṇṭum. tuṇivu uṇṭāka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, tuṇivai veḷiyiṭa vāyppu vēṇṭum. periyōrkaḷiṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. aṟivuraiyāl payaṉillai; ceytu kāṭṭalē vēṇṭappeṟuvatu.



	collutal yārkkum eḷiya; ariyavām
	colliya vaṇṇam ceyal.39
paḷḷiyiṉ aṉṟāṭa nikaḻccikaḷil ciṟuvarkaḷiṭam payaṉpaṭum pala paḻakkaṅkaḷai ēṟpaṭuttalām; vaḷarac ceyyalām. paṭṭaṟivu mikamika māṇākkarkaḷ tam maṉaccāṉṟiṟkup poruntum ceyalkaḷaiyē ceyya viḻaikiṉṟaṉar. taṅkaḷ naṭattaiyaiyum piṟar naṭattaiyaiyum matippiṭavum muyalkiṉṟaṉar.
viḷaiyāṭukaḷattil nalloḻukka vaḷarccikkup pala vāyppukkaḷ taralām. ovvoruvakai viḷaiyāṭṭiṟkum talaimaip poṟuppu māṇākkarkaḷukkē taralām. palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷukkut tērtal muṟaiyil māṇākkarkaḷait tērnteṭuttu avarkaḷiṭam pala poṟuppukkaḷai oppa ṭaikkalām. paḷḷi viḷaiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ naṭai peṟuṅkāl itaṟkup pala vāyppukkaḷ eḻukiṉṟaṉa.
paḷḷiyiṉ kuḻūuk kiḷarcci40 viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷālum, paḷḷiviḻākkaḷālum, ilakkiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷālum, palvēṟu pōṭṭikaḷālum, āciriyariṉ ceyalkaḷālum vaḷarkkap peṟukiṉṟatu. nāṭṭuppaṟṟu, carvatēcappaṟṟu, vāḻkkai nōkkam, cakōtarattuvam, camatarumam, camūkat toṇṭu pōṉṟa paṇpukaḷ paḷḷiyiltāṉ vaḷarkkappeṟutal vēṇṭum. piṟkālattil māṇākkariṉ muḻumaiyāṉa vāḻkkaiyiṉ nōkkattaip paḷḷi ōraḷavu niṟaivēṟṟa vēṇṭum. mēṟ kūṟiyavai niṟaivēṟāviṭil, paḷḷi etaic cātittālum payaṉillai. oḻukkamē vāḻkkaikku viḻuppam taruvatu; atuvē vāḻkkaiyiṉ uyirnāṭi eṉpataip payiṟṟuvōr eṉṟum niṉaivil vaittup paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum.
oḻukka vaḷarcciyil paḷḷiyiṉ muyaṟciyaiyum vīṭṭiṉ muyaṟciyaiyum iyaipuṟuttal
paḷḷiyum vīṭum iraṇṭu ciṟanta kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷ. oḻukkam mikacciṟiya paruvattilēyē amaivatāl, atil vīṭu atikap paṅkiṉaip peṟukiṉṟatu. māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyil paḷḷiyil kaḻiyum nēramē atikam eṉpatai evarum aṟivar. eṉavē, vīṭṭil naṭaipeṟum nikaḻccikaḷ avaṉ paḷḷiyil peṟum kalviyaip pātikkac ceykiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, paḷḷi iyaṟṟa virumpum viṉaikaḷaṉaittaiyum tāṉākavē cātittu viṭa muṭiyātu. oḻukkam, āḷumai, kuṭimai ākiya paṇpukaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṭam vaḷarppataṟkum, māṇākkarkaḷiṭam virumpattakka paḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum maṉappāṉmaikaḷaiyum uṇṭākkuvataṟkum, peṟṟōriṉ uṭkaruttai yaṟintu kuḻantaikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyil avarkaḷiṉ tuṇaiyaip peṟuvataṟkum paḷḷi taṉ vēlaiyai vīṭṭu vēlaiyuṭaṉ iyaipu paṭutta vēṇṭum; iraṇṭum iṇaintu neruṅkip piṇaintu pōka vaḻivakaikaḷai vakukka vēṇṭum.
peṟṟōriṉ uṭkaruttum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ ceyalaip purintu koḷḷa virumpum āciriyarukkup peritum payaṉpaṭum. kuḻantaiyiṉ uṭalnilai, kuḻantai vāḻum illattiṉ cūḻ nilai, aṅkuḷḷa kavarccikaḷ, cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnilaikaḷ, vīṭṭil avaṉuṭaiya naṭattai mutaliyavaṟṟai āciriyar naṉku aṟintu koṇṭāltāṉ āciriyarum tam toḻilait tiṟam paṭac ceyya muṭiyum. āciriyar aṭikkaṭi kuḻantaikaḷiṉ illaṅkaḷaip pārvaiyiṭutal, peṟṟōruṭaṉ uḷḷaṅ kalantu iṉṉurai yāṭutal pōṉṟavaṟṟālum, peṟṟōruṭaṉ iṇaintu iyaṟṟum ceyalkaḷ mūlamum māṇākkarkaḷpaṟṟiya pala piracciṉaikaḷait tīrttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. peṟṟōrkaḷ tam contak kuḻantaiyaip paṟṟi atikamākat terintu koḷḷum aṟivuṭaiyavarāka iruttal vēṇṭum. uṭal vaḷarcciyil tām kāṭṭum akkaṟaiyaip pōl uḷḷa vaḷarcciyilum tamakkup paṅku uṇṭu eṉpatai ovvoru peṟṟōrum uṇartal vēṇṭum. uṇara muṭiyāta peṟ ṟōrkaḷuṭaṉ āciriyarkaḷ uraiyāṭik kalantu pēci, avarkaḷ uṇarumpaṭic ceyyalām. 
māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyil paḷḷiyum āciriyarum mēṟkoḷḷum ceyalkaḷ aṉaittaiyum peṟṟōrkaḷ naṉku aṟital vēṇṭum. peṟṟōr-āciriyar kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, taṉi uraiyāṭalkaḷ, aṟikkaikaḷ, vīṭukaḷukku aṉuppum kuṟippukkaḷ, paḻaiya māṇākkark kaḻakak kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, tērcci aṟikkaikaḷ mutaliyavai aṉaittum innōkkattai niṟaivēṟṟuvataṟku mēṟkoḷḷappeṟum vāyilkaḷ ākum. ivai ovvoṉṟaiyum peṟṟōr purintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. puriyāta peṟṟōr kaṭkup puriya vaikka vēṇṭiyatu āciriyarkaḷiṉ kaṭamai. kuḻantaikaḷiṉ vaḷarcci ciṟanta muṟaiyil pātukākkap peṟavēṇṭumeṉpataṟkākavē camūkam āciriyarkaṭkut taṉip payiṟci aḷittu varukiṉṟatu eṉpatai āciriyarkaḷ uṇartal vēṇṭum.
nilaitta payaṉaip peṟavēṇṭumāyiṉ, paḷḷi toṭaṅkum nalviṉaikaḷ aṉaittum vīṭṭilum toṭarntu naṭaipeṟa vacatikaḷ aḷittal vēṇṭum. uṭalnilai, pala mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya uṭalnala vaḻikaḷuṭaṉ, nalla nūlkaḷ, paruva itaḻkaḷ, viḷaiyāṭṭuc cāmāṉkaḷ mutaliyavai vīṭṭilum kiṭaikka vēṇṭum. vīṭṭup poṟuppukkaḷ, kaṭamaikaḷ, paṇattaik kaiyāḷutal, kāykaṟic cantai, maḷikaikkaṭai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu varutal pōṉṟa poṟuppukkaḷaiyum cila camayam kuḻantaikaṭku aḷittal naṟpayaṉ viḷaivikkum. ivai paḷḷi vēlaikkup peruntuṇaiyākavum amaiyum.
kuḻantaikaḷaik kālā kālattil paḷḷikku aṉuppuvatu, paḷḷiyil tarappeṟum vīṭṭu vēlaiyai (hṭiana-ṟaṭicama)c carivarac ceyyac colluvatu. paḷḷi oḻuṅku muṟaikaḷai kavaṉikkac ceyvatu pōṉṟavaṟṟil peṟṟōrkaḷ āciriyarukkup peruntuṇaiyāka iruttal vēṇṭum. kuḻantai vaḷaruṅkāl ataṉ oḻukkattaip pātikkum nikaḻccikaḷ vīṭṭilum eḻalām, paḷḷiyilum eḻalām. vīṭṭil naṭaipeṟupavai paḷḷi naṭattaiyaip pātikkum; paḷḷiyil naṭaipeṟupavai vīṭṭu naṭattaiyait tākkalām. peṟṟōr-āciriyar kūṭṭuṟavu, ittakaiyavaṟṟaip purintu koḷḷa vāyppukkaḷ nalkum. 



iyal-12
matippīṭum cōtaṉaiyum
kuḻantaikaḷ paḷḷikaḷilum paḷḷikku veḷiyilum kaṟṟavaṟṟai aḷantaṟiyap payaṉpaṭuttum cōtaṉaikaḷaik1 kalvit tuṟai āyvukaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭalām. āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpittalāl kuḻantaikaḷ aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟaiyum, vakuppaṟai, paḷḷi aṉupavaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟaiyum, paḷḷikku veḷiyil peṟum aṉupavaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟaiyum taṉittaṉiyākap pirittu aṟivatum, ovvoru vakaiyilum aṟintukoṇṭatu evvaḷavu eṉpataik kaṇakkiṭṭu aṟivatum iyalāta kāriyam. eṉavē, ellāt tuṟaikaḷilum kaṟṟavaṟṟai oru cērattāṉ aṟintukoḷḷa iyalum. māṇākkaṉ evaṟṟaik kaṟṟirukkiṉṟāṉ, evvaḷavu naṉṟākak kaṟṟirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu aṟutiyiṭuvatutāṉ matippiṭutal2 eṉpatu.
ciṟitu kālamākak kalvipaṟṟi mēṟkoḷḷappeṟṟu varum ārāyccikaḷil oṉṟu tērvukaḷaip3 paṟṟiya ārāycciyākum. nīṇṭa kālamākap paḷḷikaḷil mēṟkoḷḷappeṟum tērvu kaḷiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷaippaṟṟiyum, avai enta aḷavu muṟṟup peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉpataip paṟṟiyum pala nāṭukaḷil ārāyccikaḷ naṭaipeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉākap pala putiya āyvukaḷum karuttukkaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
tērvukaḷiṉ nōkkam: payiṟṟaliṉ payaṉai aḷantaṟiyap payaṉpaṭupavai tērvukaḷ. eṉavē, avai toṉṟutoṭṭu naṭaipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. vakuppu māṟṟam, paḷḷikkaṭṭaṇac calukai, toḻilakaṅkaḷukkum aluvalakaṅ kaḷukkum āṭkaḷaip poṟukki eṭuttal ākiyavaṟṟiṟku avai mēṟkoḷḷap peṟuvatai nām aṟivōm. tērvukaḷiṉṟi māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tērcciyai uttēcamāka aṟutiyiṭuvatu virumpattakkataṉṟu.  pala iṭaṅkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ payilukiṉṟaṉar; pala paḷḷikaḷil payilukiṉṟaṉar.  pala āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpikkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, potut tērvukaḷ iṉṟiyamai yātavai.
tērvukaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉaiyum kaṟṟavaṟṟaiyum matippiṭuvataṟku ēṟpaṭṭa kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉ amainta uṟṟu nōkkal4 ākum.  orē kaṭṭuppāṭāṉa cūḻnilaiyil ellōraiyum orē kālattil matippiṭuvataṟkut tērvukaḷ tuṇai ceykiṉṟaṉa.
4kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉ amaintu uṟṟu nōkkal-uṭivēcaṭiṭaṭanaṉa ṭiḷenacaeyavaiṭin.
payilvōrukkum tērvukaḷ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  avai māṇākkarkaḷai ūṉṟip paṭikkat tūṇṭit tiṭṭamāṉa aṟivaip peṟac ceykiṉṟaṉa.  paḷḷi vāḻkkaiyilum puṟa vāḻkkaiyilum oruvita oḻuṅkiṉai ēṟpaṭutti virumpattakka vakaiyil pōṭṭi  maṉappāṉmaiyai uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa.  tērvukaḷil payaṉākap payilvōr tam aṟiviṉ kuṟaipāṭukaḷai aṟiyavum, payiṟṟuvōr tam payiṟṟaliṉ kuṟainiṟaikaḷai aṟiyavum muṭikiṉṟatu.  eṉavē, tērvukaḷ paṇṭutoṭṭu iṉṟuvarai iṉṟiyamaiyāta tollaiyākavē iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟu iruvita āyvukaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyil iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.  oṉṟu, kaṭṭurai āyvukaḷ, maṟṟoṉṟu, putiya muṟai āyvukaḷ, vāymoḻi āyvukaḷum5 cila camayam mēṟkoḷḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
kaṭṭurai āyvukaḷ6
kaṭṭurai āyvai nām naṉku aṟivōm.  itu pal lāṇṭukaḷāka naṭai muṟaiyil iruntu varuvatu; mūṉṟu allatu iraṇṭarai maṇikkāla aḷavil aintu allatu āṟu viṉākkaḷaik kēṭṭu oru pāṭam muḻuvatilum māṇākkaṉukku irukka vēṇṭiya oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟāṇṭu aṟivai aḷantaṟiya muyalkiṉṟatu inta āyvu.  putu muṟai āyvukaḷ celvākkup peṟṟa piṟaku itaṉ celvākku kuṟaiyat toṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu.  eṉiṉum, inta āyvai aṭiyōṭu nīkkavaḻiyē illai; kallūri vakuppukkaḷil itutāṉ iṉṟum āṭci celutti varukiṉṟatu.  pālārṭu7 pōṉṟa kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ ivvāyvu cariyāṉa aḷavuk karuviyalla eṉpataip pallāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē meyppittuviṭṭaṉar.  itiluḷḷa kuṟaikaḷaiyum niṟaikaḷaiyum naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.  avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavaṟṟai īṇṭu kūṟuvōm.
kuṟaikaḷ : (1) inta āyvukaḷāl aḷantaṟiyakkūṭiya poruḷaṟivu kuṟaivu.  ciṟiya kāla aḷavil āṇṭu muḻuvatum paṭittaṟinta pāṭa aṟivai aintāṟu viṉākkaḷāl tēṟiviṭa muṭiyum eṉṟu eṇṇuvatu poruntātavoṉṟu.  oru viṉāvait tappākap purintu koḷpavar allatu avviṭaiyiṉ pakuti kaṟpikkappaṭṭapoḻutu paḷḷikku vārātiruntavar cariyāka viṭaiyiṟukka muṭiyātu pōkak kūṭum.
(2) āyvāḷarkaḷ viṭaiyēṭukaḷai orē potu nōkkamum orē vita nōkkamum koṇṭu tiruttukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkillai.  viṭaikaḷiṉ takutiyaiyaṟintu ēṟṟa matip peṇkaḷaik koṭuppatu āyvāḷarkaḷiṉ maṉanilaiyaiyē poṟuttirukkiṉṟatu.  mukkuṇavacattāl muṟai piṟaḻntu pōvatuṭaṉ nillātu, ciṉam, makiḻcci, mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa matippeṇkaḷ kuṟaintum mikuntum pōkakkūṭum.
(3) kaṭṭuṟai eṉpatu pala kūṟukaḷaik koṇṭa tiṟaṉāl uṇṭākkap peṟṟatoru paṭaippu. ovvoruvarum tattamak kēṟṟavāṟu aḷavukōlkaḷai māṟṟikkoṇṭu matippiṭuvar.
(4) ivvāyvāl peṟum matippeṇkaḷ nampat takuntavaiyalla; māṟupāṭillātavaiyumalla.  naṉku matippiṭa vēṇṭumāyiṉ atika kālam ākum.  poruḷaṟivu illai yāyiṉum coṟtiṟamaiyāl atika matippeṇkaḷ peṟutal kūṭum.
niṟaikaḷ : (1) āyvukaḷai āyattam ceyvatum eḷitu; kaiyāḷuvatum eḷitu.  viṉākkaḷukku evvāṟu viṭaiyiṟuppatu eṉpatu paṟṟit tērvāḷar māṇākkarkaṭku viḷakkat tēvai illai.
(2) paḷḷik kalvi ēṟpāṭṭiluḷḷa ellāp pāṭaṅkaṭkum immuṟai āyvukaḷai eḷitil kaiyāḷalām.
(3) poruḷkaḷiṉ varaiyaṟaivukaḷ kūṟuvatu, teḷivu paṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu, tokuppatu, vakuppatu, virippatu, curukkuvatu mutaliya tiṟaṉkaḷaiyellām ivvakai āyvukaḷāltāṉ aḷantaṟiya iyalum.
putiya muṟai āyvukaḷ
putiya muṟai āyvukaḷ8 eṉpavai oru taṉiyāḷiṉ paṇpukaḷai oru eṇ matippāl viḷakkuvataṟkeṉṟu āyntu tiṭṭamiṭappeṟṟa nilaimaiyākum.  ivaṟṟil kiṭṭattaṭṭa muppattaintukku mēṟpaṭṭa vakaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  eṉṟālum, ivaṟṟai aṟiñarkaḷ mīṭṭaṟital vakai (canauṭipanvaiiyīn valayīna), niṉaivukūr vakai (canauyaṭaṭa valayīna) eṉa iru kūṟiṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
mīṭṭaṟital vakaiyil, cari-tavaṟu āyvukaḷ, poṟukku āyvukaḷ, iṇaikkum āyvukaḷ mutaliyavai aṭaṅkum; niṉaivukūrvakaiyil nirappu āyvukaḷ mutaliyavai aṭaṅkum.  āyvut tuṟaiyil muṉṉēṟṟam aṭaiya aṭaiya, aṟiñarkaḷ putiyavakaikaḷaikkaṇṭu koṇṭē irukkiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē, ivvakaikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyum vaḷarntu koṇṭē yirukkiṉṟatu.  āṉāl, ēḻu allatu eṭṭu vakai āyvukaḷē naṭaimuṟaiyil payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
cari-tavaṟu āyvukaḷ9 : cila kuṟitta toṭar moḻikaḷaik koṭuttu avaṟṟuḷ varum karuttukkaḷ cariyāṉavaiyā, tavaṟāṉavaiyā eṉpataṟkēṟpak kākkaippuḷḷiyaiyō cuḻiyaiyō iṭumpaṭi kūṟutal.  ivaṟṟaiyē viṉāvāka amaittu ‘ām,’ ‘illai’, ‘teriyātu’, eṉṟa viṭaikaḷil oṉṟai eḻutumāṟum kūṟalām.
sae-ṭu]aṭiyiṟkāṇum coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟil eḻuttup piḻaikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa cariyāṉavaṟṟiṟku  nērāka \ eṉṟum, piḻaiyāṉavaṟṟiṟku  nērāka o eṉṟum kuṟikaḷai iṭuka.

3. kōtumpai		7. vāḷaippaḻam
4. kōttāṉ		8. viyāḷaṉ
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai.
palviṭaiyiṟ poṟukku āyvukaḷ10: muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟai viṭa ivai caṟṟu arumaiyum vaḷarcciyum uṭaiyavai.  ivaṟṟuḷ oru poruḷpaṟṟiya pala toṭar moḻikaḷil etu cari eṉṟu māṇākkarkaḷ oṉṟait tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.  ovvoru viṉāvukkum  mūṉṟiliruntu aintu varait toṭar moḻikaḷ koṭukkappeṟum,

nirappum āyvukaḷ11: kōṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟṟa coṟkaḷai amaittut toṭarmoḻiyai muṭittukkāṭṭal.  ivvakai āyvukaḷ kīḻvakuppu māṇākkarkaṭku mikavum ēṟṟavai.  ēṟṟa collai eḻutuvataṟku muṉ oru muṟai toṭarmoḻiyaip paṭittup piṉ collai
amaittup piṉṉum oru muṟai toṭarmoḻiyaip paṭittut toṭar moḻi cariyāṉa poruḷaik koṭukkiṉṟatā eṉṟu pārkkumpaṭi aṟivuṟutta vēṇṭum.

iṇaikkum āyvukaḷ12; iraṇṭu varicaikaḷākat toṭarmoḻikaḷai uṇmaiyum poruttamum muṟaiyum piṟaḻa amaittu avaṟṟaip poruttumpaṭi ceytal iṇaikkum āyvukaḷākum.  nikaḻccikaḷ, avai nikaḻnta yāṇṭukaḷ; pōrkaḷ, avaṟṟai naṭattiya cēṉait talaivarkaḷ, pulavarkaḷ, avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ; paḻamoḻikaḷ avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷ; maraput toṭarkaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟai ivvāṟu poruttac ceyyalām. kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷaiyum varicaippaṭuttalām.  kuṟitta poruḷaṟivait tēṟa itu ciṟanta muṟaiyākum.
sae-ṭu]  kīḻkkaṇṭavaṟṟil a piriviluḷḷavaṟṟai ā piriviluḷḷa vaṟṟuṭaṉ poruttik kāṭṭuka.  pacura vaḷaiviṟkuḷ viṭaiyiṉ eṇṇai maṭṭilum iṭṭāl pōtumāṉatu.
